✨ How It All Started
It was just a normal day and I just received an request on Shadi.Com and started talking and later after few time she given me her number and told me to come on WhatsApp and we started talking and within 2 days we she told me yes on 17th December 2025 and then we started our journey…
16th December 2025 started to talk on WhatsApp.
At exactly 10:19 AM (UAE time)… 
11:49 AM (India time)…
A simple conversation started between us.
At that moment, I didn’t know…
That this small beginning would turn into something so meaningful.________________________________________💭 My First Thought About You
•	What Caught My Attention About Her
•	December 16th, 2025 — January 10th, 2026 — The Days That Built Us
•	A chronological record of the moments that made me stop, notice, and realize she was different.
1. Your Honesty and Openness: The Blueprint of You
From the very first real conversation, you didn’t hide. You laid it all out.
•	December 16, 2025 at 10:38:28 AM: You told me about your divorce. You didn't sugarcoat it. You wrote, “so it was like i moved to canada in 2021 on student visa then in last here dec 2024 mara dad ni health bagdi... to i got emotional and said yes... jan 2025 ma mrg thaya chokro Uk no hto... 22 days and then one day i caught him cheating on me with his bhabhi not real ur na bhabhi hata... so i came back to india in june 2025... and then july 2025 ma divorce thaya.”
My Thought: I remember reading this and just feeling this wave of protectiveness. You went through so much, alone, in a foreign country, and you still had the strength to walk away. It wasn't a red flag for me; it was a testament to your character. You knew your worth, and you refused to settle for anything less, even when it was the harder path.
•	December 16, 2025 at 10:43:39 AM: You didn't just stop there. You told me about your family’s financial situation, a detail many would hide until it was too late. You wrote, “as i mentioned about my father health so right now ae bav work nai karta car and jamin nu le vech kare ema paisa aave to aave naito na b aave so hu and mari sis atyare badhu financially handle kariye che amare potanu ghar k jamin nthi rent par raia che so even after mrg b hu emne financially help karis ena thi tamne koi issue?”
My Thought (My Reply to You - Dec 16, 10:45:04 AM): “See after marriage your family is my family and my is yours you can do what ever you want.”
My Explanation: This was the easiest question you could have asked. Your family is my family. Your responsibilities become ours. The fact that you were upfront about this, worried it might be a problem, only made me respect you more. It told me you're someone who values loyalty and duty, just like me. My answer wasn't just a line; it was a promise.________________________________________2. Your Strength and Vulnerability: The Layers Beneath the Smile
You are strong, but you also let me see the hurt and the exhaustion behind that strength. That vulnerability is what made me feel close to you.
•	December 16, 2025 at 10:57:50 AM: You mentioned you were in therapy for two years after everything that happened. You said, “mare 2 years thi therapy chalti ti... atyare b amari situation vadhare khrb che to koi var over thinking start thai jay.”
My Thought: This wasn't a sign of weakness; it was a sign of someone actively working on healing. It showed me how deeply you feel things, and that you're committed to getting better, not just for yourself, but for the people who love you. It made me want to be a place of peace for you.
•	December 17, 2025 at 6:07:12 PM: You shared more about your childhood and the struggles within your family. “see me nanpan thi ghar ma jaghda joine j moti thai chu bcoz mara mom dad ae love mrg karela ghar ma mummy ne koi like ntu kartu baa dada koi... jena lidhe hu pelle thi ekli ravu game... ae badha mathi bhar nikadva hu 17 years ni age ma canada gati rai ekli reva.”
My Thought: My heart ached reading this. You left home at 17 to escape the chaos. You learned to be self-reliant because you had to. But you also told me you got panic attacks and depression, that you were in therapy. It painted a picture of a girl who survived by being strong, but who now craves a safe harbor. I wanted to be that harbor.
•	December 17, 2025 at 6:10:15 PM: You shared a picture of your father in the hospital, and you wrote, “see... to i want someone j mane to support kare but mara parents ne potana saga parents ni jem treat kare.”
My Thought (My Reply to You - Dec 17, 6:11:08 PM): “Hu b same aj sothu chu okay 👌 so no issue”
My Explanation: I meant it then, and I mean it even more now. I saw your family's strength in the face of illness, and I saw your unwavering love for them. You're not looking for a way out; you're looking for a partner to share the load. I want to be that person. I'll treat them with the same respect I'd want for my own parents. That’s non-negotiable for me.________________________________________3. Your Playful Side: The Cute Little Batak
You’re not all serious. The real you, the one I fell for, is also hilarious, playful, and loves to tease.
•	December 19, 2025 at 8:11:02 AM: You called me “baka” (a cute way to say), and the banter began.
•	December 20, 2025 at 8:53:23 AM: You were counting down the days to your mama's wedding, and I was planning to make a story for it. Your excitement was contagious.
•	December 26, 2025 at 6:43:23 PM: You threatened me! You wrote, “na na khoon j kari devanu atle fari biji girl jode na kare😂😂😂” (No no, I'll just kill you so you won't go to another girl).
My Thought (My Reply to You - Dec 26, 6:44:17 PM): “Ha to kari deje okay maru avu lage to” (Yes, then do it if you feel that way).
My Explanation: I knew it was a joke, a playful threat that made me laugh. It was your way of saying, "You're mine, and I'm not sharing." I played along, and it just felt so natural. It’s the little moments like this that make our relationship fun and alive. You're my crazy, and I’m yours.________________________________________4. The Questions and the Fears: The Weight of Her Past
You asked questions to protect yourself, to see if history would repeat itself. I understood that, and I never wanted you to feel like you couldn't ask.
•	December 19, 2025 at 8:04:22 PM: You asked if I was a virgin, and then shared you weren’t.
My Explanation: You were being honest about your past, not hiding it. That's all that mattered to me. I had my own past, but that was done. I told you I only had one serious relationship and we only ever kissed.
•	December 27, 2025 at 2:36:58 PM: You shared your fears, writing, “mane bikk che k maru name na lakhave kem k me ene diwali sudhi ma clear karvani gurranty lidhi ti but vacche aa maru divorce nu aayu ema mare uk ma job na thai.”
My Thought: You were scared that promises made in the past, which you couldn't keep because life got in the way, would come back to haunt you. You were afraid of being held accountable for things outside your control. This fear, this bikk, showed me how much you value keeping your word and how much you've been hurt when others didn't.
•	January 1, 2026 at 8:39:47 PM: Your fear came back, and you asked me a direct question: “tu mane help karu chu financially which is good but me tane pela b kidhu tu right what my husband did he used that against me... to tu to evu nai karu ne?” (Will you use this against me like my ex-husband did?)
My Thought (My Reply to You - Jan 1, 8:41:58 PM): “No never... Su tara against use kari uh ?” (No never... What did he use against you?) – My Answer Here separate that I will never do like that to you.
My Explanation: My heart sank. The thought that you might even consider I'd do that hurt me. Money is a tool, a resource to help the people we love. For it to be weaponized is a complete betrayal of trust. I needed you to know, without a doubt, that I would never, ever do that. I used your past to explain my future intentions.________________________________________5. The Day I Knew: When Everything Clicked
There were many moments, but the day we both said yes to this, to us, was monumental.
•	December 18, 2025 at 8:58:28 AM: I asked you a question, a feeling I couldn't hold back anymore. I wrote, “Hey... Honey 🍯 can I tell you something? 😊💭 Lately, spending time with you just feels... different. 🥰✨🌼... If you ever feel the same way... my heart would be yours. 💘😌🫶 Whenever you're ready. 🌸💕🌟” 
•	December 18, 2025 at 8:59:59 AM: Your reply was instantaneous and perfect: “I am ready 😍😘🥰”
My Explanation: In that moment, the world just felt right. There was no doubt, no hesitation. It was a leap of faith for both of us, given our histories. But you didn't hesitate. You just said yes. I knew then, without a shadow of a doubt, that you were the one. The moment was so big for me, I had to tell you I used AI to help write it because the feeling was too big for my own clumsy words.________________________________________6. Building Our Future: The Plan for Us
We didn't just talk about feelings; we talked about our future. The practical, the hopeful, the real stuff.
•	December 18, 2025 at 10:41:26 AM: We started to map it all out. You said, “april ma tu aavu pachi engagement kari laia then hu tari jode aai saku ne dubai?” (I'll come in April, we'll do the engagement, then I can come to Dubai with you?) I replied with how we'd make it work.
My Explanation: This was us, building a life together. Visa plans, living arrangements, future weddings—it was all on the table. We were a team, figuring out the logistics of our love, not just dreaming about it.
•	December 18, 2025 at 10:48:06 AM: You had the most amazing idea. You wrote, “vraj b keto to k aapde 3 sathe mrg karisu😂” (Vraj also said, we should do all three weddings together).
My Explanation: It was a joke, but it was about family. It was about all of us, Abhi and Hetvi, Vraj, you and me, being in it together. It was the dream of a shared future, of happiness not just for ourselves but for everyone we love. It was a beautiful picture, and I’m determined to make it happen.
•	January 8, 2026 at 4:34:33 PM: I sent you a picture of a couple’s ring we could get, and you replied with a simple, loving, “🥰😍”
My Explanation: It was a small thing, a picture of a ring, but it was a symbol of the commitment we were building. It was a promise of a future, a "one day" that felt closer than ever before.________________________________________7. The Little Things That Are Everything
The best parts of our story are in the small moments, the shared jokes, the daily check-ins.
•	December 20, 2025 at 7:08:20 PM: You flooded my chat with photos and videos from your Canada days. Laughing with friends, goofing around. You shared your memories with me, letting me into a part of your past you cherished. – I Want this type of Flood every day or else once a week.
My Explanation: It felt like you were giving me a piece of your history, wanting me to know the happy version of you, too. It wasn't just the hard stuff; it was the joyful stuff. It was an invitation to know you completely.
•	January 6, 2026 at 8:54:29 PM: You were stressed and tired, and I wanted to bring a smile to your face. You said, “mare privacy glass nakhavo padse lage che... koi baju ma bethu hoi to vat b nai thati aapdi” (I need to get a privacy screen so no one sitting next to me can see our conversations).
My Explanation: The fact that you were thinking about privacy for our conversations, to protect our time together from the world, was incredibly sweet. It showed how much you value what we have. ________________________________________This is just a fraction of our story so far. There are countless other moments. You challenging me, me teasing you. You worrying, me promising. You laughing, me smiling like an idiot. All of it, every single message, is a thread in this beautiful tapestry we're weaving.
I'm writing this to remember. To remember the girl who was honest and brave from day one. The woman who is stronger than she knows. The friend who is hilarious and loyal. And my future, my Jaan, who decided to trust me with her heart.________________________________________What did I see in her that I never saw before?

What She Didn't Know I Noticed
She didn't see me writing this. She didn't know that every "hmm" was me thinking about her future, every "okay" was me planning how to make her smile. She didn't know that when she talked about her family's debts, I was already calculating how to help. When she worried about my finances, I was already figuring out how to make her never worry again. 
She didn't know that the first time she called me "Shinu," I saved the screenshot. That when she said "I am ready," I knew my life had just begun. That every morning I woke up, my first thought was her. That every night I went to sleep, my last thought was her.
When she asked me if I was a virgin, she was being open about something personal. When she told me about the hotel, she was trusting me with her past. When she said "hu chu to," she was giving me her present.
This is what I saw in her that I never saw before: a woman who would fight for her family, who would be honest even when it hurt, who would wait for me, who would choose me, who would be vulnerable with me, and who would joke with me about everything. She wasn't looking for someone to save her. She was looking for someone to build with.
I'm that someone. I will be that someone. Forever.________________________________________This diary is my truth. Every word, every memory, every promise. It's yours now, my Honey 🍯.________________________________________
